# Skills · M · part 88

901 entries, Meraki Devices to merge. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Meraki Devices**: Cisco Meraki device inventory and status through the hosted Meraki MCP: serial-based identity, the MX/MS/MR/MV/MG/MT product lines, org inventory versus network assignment, availability and its change history, uplink addresses, provisioning, end-of-life (EOX) and firmware status. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/meraki/meraki/skills/devices) · ★55)
- **Meraki Security Appliance**: Cisco Meraki MX security appliance review through the hosted Meraki MCP: L3, L7 and inbound firewall rules, port forwarding and 1:1 / 1:many NAT, firewalled services, organization-wide policy rulesets, site-to-site VPN status, VPN firewall rules, third-party peers, threat protection and security events, and SSID L3 firewall rules. Changes are produced as an exact proposed ruleset diff for a human to apply. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/meraki/meraki/skills/security-appliance) · ★55)
- **Meraki Troubleshooting**: Read-only Cisco Meraki diagnostics through the hosted Meraki MCP: site and device outages from availability history and WAN uplink loss/latency, switch port status and error counters, LLDP/CDP topology, wireless connection failures and packet loss, client lookup by MAC, and "what changed?" from the change log and event log. Live tests are handed to a human in the Dashboard. ([WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins](https://github.com/WYRE-AI/msp-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/msp-claude-plugins/meraki/meraki/skills/troubleshooting) · ★55)
